Line endings. The repo had no .gitattributes and the local git config had\r\n   core.autocrlf=true, so a checkout rewrote every text file to CRLF on disk.\r\n   The git OBJECTS were already LF-clean (verified with `git cat-file blob`),\r\n   so the CRLF was introduced by the checkout, not by the source of truth. Two\r\n   builds of the same commit therefore shipped different bytes for 7 members.\r\n\r\n2. Archive timestamps. zip records the mtime of each file it packs. Two builds\r\n   minutes apart produced 39 differing timestamps, so the member digests could\r\n   never match even when the content did.\r\n\r\nThis tool removes both causes instead of documenting them:\r\n\r\n  * the source tree is exported with `git -c core.autocrlf=false archive`, so the\r\n    build input is the git blob content (LF), never the working tree;\r\n  * SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH is pinned, so the archive timestamps are fixed.\r\n\r\nMEASURED RESULT: two independent builds of a6bcdd0 through this tool produced\r\nthe identical wheel sha256 and a single distinct member timestamp.\r\n\r\nThe tool NEVER builds from a dirty working tree: it always exports a named\r\ncommit, so a build is a function of (commit, builder version) only.\r\n\r\nUsage:\r\n    python tools/build_reproducible.py --commit a6bcdd0 --outdir dist\r\n    python tools/build_reproducible.py --commit HEAD --outdir dist --verify\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport gzip\r\nimport hashlib\r\nimport io\r\nimport json\r\nimport os\r\nimport shutil\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport sys\r\nimport tarfile\r\nimport zipfile\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\n# A fixed, arbitrary epoch (2023-11-14T22:13:20Z). Pinned so the archive\r\n# timestamps are constant across builds and machines.\r\nDEFAULT_SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH = 1700000000\r\n\r\n\r\ndef run(cmd: list[str], **kw) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\r\n    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, **kw)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef run_bytes(cmd: list[str], **kw) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\r\n    \"\"\"Same as run() but keeps stdout as bytes. `git archive` emits a binary tar.\"\"\"\r\n    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, **kw)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _has_build(python: str) -> bool:\r\n    \"\"\"Can this interpreter actually run the build backend?\r\n\r\n    NOT `import build`. A repository that contains a `build/` directory (this one\r\n    does: `build/bdist.win-amd64`, `build/lib`) makes `import build` succeed as a\r\n    NAMESPACE PACKAGE, so the naive probe returns True for an interpreter that\r\n    cannot build anything. The independent checker found this: the brief's own\r\n    command failed with \"No module named build.__main__; 'build' is a package and\r\n    cannot be directly executed\", while the resolver had reported success.\r\n\r\n    The probe must therefore ask the question that will actually be asked later:\r\n    can this interpreter RUN the build module?\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    r = subprocess.run(\r\n        [python, \"-m\", \"build\", \"--version\"], capture_output=True, timeout=180\r\n    )\r\n    if r.returncode == 0:\r\n        return True\r\n    # `python -m build` needs a console entry point; fall back to importing the\r\n    # module's __main__, which is the thing the resolver actually invokes.\r\n    r2 = subprocess.run(\r\n        [python, \"-c\", \"import build.__main__\"], capture_output=True, timeout=120\r\n    )\r\n    return r2.returncode == 0\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _builder_python() -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Find an interpreter that can actually run `python -m build`.\r\n\r\n    The build backend must not depend on whatever `sys.executable` happens to be:\r\n    the independent checker runs this tool from a venv that does NOT have\r\n    `build` installed, and the build then fails for an environmental reason that\r\n    has nothing to do with the artefact. Resolution order:\r\n\r\n      1. the interpreter running this script, if it has `build`;\r\n      2. `$SBL_BUILD_PYTHON`, if it has `build`;\r\n      3. any interpreter on PATH that has `build` (checked in order);\r\n      4. any venv interpreter next to the repository.\r\n\r\n    If none has it, the failure says exactly that instead of a bare\r\n    \"No module named build\".\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    override = os.environ.get(\"SBL_BUILD_PYTHON\")\r\n    candidates: list[str] = []\r\n    if override:\r\n        candidates.append(override)\r\n    candidates.append(sys.executable)\r\n    for name in (\"python\", \"python3\", \"py\"):\r\n        found = shutil.which(name)\r\n        if found:\r\n            candidates.append(found)\r\n    repo = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\r\n    for venv in (repo / \".venv\" / \"Scripts\" / \"python.exe\",\r\n                 repo.parent / \".venv\" / \"Scripts\" / \"python.exe\"):\r\n        if venv.exists():\r\n            candidates.append(str(venv))\r\n    seen: set[str] = set()\r\n    for cand in candidates:\r\n        if cand in seen:\r\n            continue\r\n        seen.add(cand)\r\n        if _has_build(cand):\r\n            return cand\r\n    raise SystemExit(\r\n        \"no interpreter with the 'build' package was found. Install it with \"\r\n        \"'pip install build', or point SBL_BUILD_PYTHON at an interpreter that \"\r\n        \"has it. (Checked: \"\r\n        + \", \".join(sorted(seen))\r\n        + \")\"\r\n    )\r\n\r\n\r\ndef git(repo: Path, *args: str) -> str:\r\n    r = run([\"git\", \"-C\", str(repo), *args])\r\n    if r.returncode != 0:\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"git failed: {' '.join(args)}\\n{r.stderr}\")\r\n    return r.stdout.strip()\r\n\r\n\r\ndef export_tree(repo: Path, commit: str, dest: Path) -> None:\r\n    \"\"\"Export `commit` to `dest` with autocrlf disabled so the bytes are the blobs.\"\"\"\r\n    if dest.exists():\r\n        shutil.rmtree(dest)\r\n    dest.mkdir(parents=True)\r\n    tar_bytes = run_bytes(\r\n        [\"git\", \"-C\", str(repo), \"-c\", \"core.autocrlf=false\", \"archive\", \"--format=tar\", commit]\r\n    )\r\n    if tar_bytes.returncode != 0:\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"git archive failed:\\n{tar_bytes.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}\")\r\n    tar_path = dest.parent / f\"{dest.name}.tar\"\r\n    tar_path.write_bytes(tar_bytes.stdout)\r\n    with tarfile.open(tar_path) as tf:\r\n        tf.extractall(dest)\r\n    tar_path.unlink()\r\n\r\n\r\ndef build(src: Path, outdir: Path, epoch: int) -> list[Path]:\r\n    env = dict(os.environ)\r\n    env[\"SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH\"] = str(epoch)\r\n    env[\"PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE\"] = \"1\"\r\n    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\r\n    r = run([_builder_python(), \"-m\", \"build\", \"--outdir\", str(outdir), str(src)], env=env)\r\n    if r.returncode != 0:\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"build failed:\\n{r.stdout[-4000:]}\\n{r.stderr[-4000:]}\")\r\n    wheels = sorted(outdir.glob(\"*.whl\"))\r\n    # setuptools honours SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH for the WHEEL but NOT for the sdist:\r\n    # measured, the .tar.gz carried 21 distinct member mtimes and the gzip header\r\n    # embedded the wall-clock time, so two sdist builds never matched. The sdist is\r\n    # therefore REPACKED here with a fixed mtime and gzip mtime=0.\r\n    for sdist in sorted(outdir.glob(\"*.tar.gz\")):\r\n        normalise_sdist(sdist, epoch)\r\n    return wheels + sorted(outdir.glob(\"*.tar.gz\"))\r\n\r\n\r\ndef normalise_sdist(sdist: Path, epoch: int) -> None:\r\n    \"\"\"Rewrite a .tar.gz so its member mtimes and the gzip header are constant.\r\n\r\n    PAX_FORMAT keeps a sub-second mtime in `member.pax_headers[\"mtime\"]`, and\r\n    `create_pax_header` only OVERWRITES that header when the value does not fit\r\n    the 12-digit ustar field. An integer epoch fits, so the original float\r\n    survived and the member mtimes stayed non-constant. The header is therefore\r\n    removed explicitly and the mtime is re-set as a float.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    raw = sdist.read_bytes()\r\n    inner = gzip.decompress(raw)\r\n    out = io.BytesIO()\r\n    with tarfile.open(fileobj=out, mode=\"w\", format=tarfile.PAX_FORMAT) as tf:\r\n        with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(inner)) as src:\r\n            for member in src.getmembers():\r\n                member.pax_headers = {\r\n                    k: v for k, v in member.pax_headers.items() if k != \"mtime\"\r\n                }\r\n                member.mtime = float(epoch)\r\n                member.uid = member.gid = 0\r\n                member.uname = member.gname = \"\"\r\n                f = src.extractfile(member) if member.isfile() else None\r\n                tf.addfile(member, f)\r\n    packed = io.BytesIO()\r\n    with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=packed, mode=\"wb\", compresslevel=9, mtime=0) as gz:\r\n        gz.write(out.getvalue())\r\n    sdist.write_bytes(packed.getvalue())\r\n\r\n\r\ndef describe(artifact: Path) -> dict:\r\n    h = hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\r\n    info = {\"name\": artifact.name, \"bytes\": artifact.stat().st_size, \"sha256\": h}\r\n    if artifact.suffix == \".whl\":\r\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(artifact) as z:\r\n            infos = z.infolist()\r\n            info[\"members\"] = len(infos)\r\n            info[\"distinct_timestamps\"] = sorted({i.date_time for i in infos})\r\n    return info\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main() -> int:\r\n    ap = \"source_tree\": str(src),\r\n        \"artifacts\": [describe(a) for a in artifacts],\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if args.verify:\r\n        outdir2 = outdir.parent / f\"{outdir.name}-verify\"\r\n        if --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\r\n# 4. the END-TO-END claim: two independent builds must be byte-identical\r\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\r\ndef test_source_date_epoch_is_pinned_across_processes():\r\n    \"\"\"The epoch must be a CONSTANT, not a value read from the clock.\r\n\r\n    The independent checker's MUT_D replaced `DEFAULT_SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` with\r\n    `int(time.time())`. That mutant leaves an in-process two-build comparison\r\n    GREEN, because one process reads the clock once and both builds then use the\r\n    same value. The defect only shows up ACROSS processes: two separate CLI runs\r\n    read the clock at different moments and produce different bytes.\r\n\r\n    So this test reads the epoch in TWO SEPARATE PROCESSES, more than a second\r\n    apart, and requires them to be equal. A clock-derived epoch differs; a\r\n    pinned one does not. It costs about two seconds and it is deterministic\r\n    (the values are integer seconds, so reads more than one second apart cannot\r\n    coincide).\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    import subprocess\r\n    import sys\r\n    import time\r\n\r\n    script = (\r\n        \"import importlib.util,sys;\"\r\n        \"spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('b', sys.argv[1]);\"\r\n        \"m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m);\"\r\n        \"print(m.DEFAULT_SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH)\"\r\n    )\r\n    tool = REPO / \"tools\" / \"build_reproducible.py\"\r\n\r\n    def _read() -> str:\r\n        r = subprocess.run(\r\n            [sys.executable, \"-c\", script, str(tool)],\r\n            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180, cwd=str(REPO),\r\n        )\r\n        assert r.returncode == 0, f\"reading the epoch failed: {r.stderr[-500:]}\"\r\n        return r.stdout.strip()\r\n\r\n    first = _read()\r\n    time.sleep(1.2)\r\n    second = _read()\r\n    assert first == second, (\r\n        \"the source-date epoch is not pinned: two processes one second apart read \"\r\n        f\"{first!r} and {second!r}. The build is a function of the clock, not of \"\r\n        \"the source.\"\r\n    )\r\n    # and it must be an integer, not a float of the wall clock\r\n    assert first.isdigit(), f\"the epoch is not an integer second: {first!r}\"\r\n\r\n\r\ndef test_two_independent_builds_are_byte_identical():\r\n    \"\"\"The suite must ASSERT the actual property, end to end.\r\n\r\n    The independent checker (VERIFY_SECURITY lane, R7 round 2) showed that a\r\n    mutation replacing `DEFAULT_SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` with wall-clock `time.time()`\r\n    leaves the whole suite GREEN while destroying reproducibility: the two builds\r\n    differ (wheel `f182f0cd...` vs `0d26e23b...`, `identical=false`).\r\n\r\n    That mutant survived because every other test in this file asserts a LOCAL\r\n    property (a rule is present, a normalisation changes a file, a timestamp is\r\n    constant) and none of them builds anything twice. A local property can hold\r\n    while the global property fails. This test builds twice and compares bytes.\r\n\r\n    The two builds run as SEPARATE PROCESSES, because that is how the claim is\r\n    used: an in-process comparison reuses one clock reading and cannot see a\r\n    clock-derived epoch (see test_source_date_epoch_is_pinned_across_processes).\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    import hashlib\r\n    import shutil\r\n    import subprocess\r\n    import sys\r\n    import tempfile\r\n    import unittest\r\n\r\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:\r\n        root = Path(td)\r\n        digests = []\r\n        for i in (1, 2):\r\n            out = root / f\"dist{i}\"\r\n            r = subprocess.run(\r\n                [sys.executable, str(REPO / \"tools\" / \"build_reproducible.py\"),\r\n                 \"--commit\", \"HEAD\", \"--outdir\", str(out)],\r\n                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=1800, cwd=str(REPO),\r\n            )\r\n            if r.returncode != 0:\r\n                raise unittest.SkipTest(\r\n                    \"no interpreter with the build backend is available in this \"\r\n                    f\"environment: {r.stdout[-400:]}{r.stderr[-400:]}\"\r\n                )\r\n            per = {f.name: hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for f in sorted(out.iterdir())}\r\n            assert per, f\"build {i} produced nothing\"\r\n            digests.append(per)\r\n\r\n    assert digests[0] == digests[1], (\r\n        \"two independent builds of the SAME commit produced different bytes: \"\r\n        f\"{digests[0]} != {digests[1]}. `PIPD_S4_R5S_PREVIEW4_VERIFY_CLOSE/` 38；`r5r3-evidence-return-20261016` 的 80 檔為 return 77 與 openspec 3。這些適合用固定 SHA 作外部證據，不需搬進產品主線。\\r\\n4. **名稱是 evidence 不代表沒有產品副作用。** `r5r3-closeout-evidence-20261016` 的 112 檔中 111 檔是 closeout 封包，但另一檔 `.gitattributes` 將三行 `* text=auto eol=lf`、`*.png binary`、`*.db binary` 改成一行 `* -text`。這是整個 repo 的文字處理政策變更；即使 merge-tree exit 0，也不應偽裝成純證據併入。\\r\\n5. **混有產品或文件的證據鏈須拆分。** `r6-closeout-evidence-20261016` 及其三個後繼分支均與 main 的 `tests/test_r6_doc_identity_guard.py` 發生 add/add 衝突，並帶有 `docs/install/README.md` 增量；`r5z-evidence-return-20261014` 帶回 README 衝突。應保留封包並另開必要的最小修補，不能一律用 theirs 消衝突。\\r\\n\\r\\n### PIPD-LS-SP 官方八個 skills：main 實際內容\\r\\n\\r\\n固定主線 `e95942abcb6aa07e69304607ab8afc22a74fc67d`；以下八個均存在於 `skills/<名稱>/SKILL.md`，各自宣告 version 1，並引用 input/output schemas、contract、examples、cases。完整原文已保存於 `skills-main.json`；這裡是契約摘要，不是執行成功宣告。\\r\\n\\r\\n| skill | main 上的實際契約內容 |\\r\\n|---|---|\\r\\n| `pipd-route-intake` | 接收 goal 與 source locators；拒絕空 goal、零 locators 與越界路徑；抽取 requirement atoms，產生內容定址 IntentCard，交給 PI compiler。 |\\r\\n| `pipd-authority-source` | 以 sha256、文件 locators 固定 source families；F1/F2/F3/F5/F6 為 R1，其餘 R2；產生 AuthorityBinding 與 SourceFreezeManifest，拒絕衝突或非 CURRENT 來源。 |\\r\\n| `pipd-profile-tailor` | 僅允許 LITE/STANDARD/ASSURED 與九個 canonical axes；拒絕未知或重複 axes；safety veto offline-gate success 遮蔽衝突，也不能直接宣稱其所有 17 個獨有提交均被 successor 替代，因 `git cherry main head` 全部是 `+`。\\r\\n- **`r5-s4-readme`：過時安裝說明衝突。** 只改 README（+217/−71），但與 main 分岔後落後 264 個提交，實測 README content conflict，head 沒有 check-runs；不得拿舊說明覆蓋主線。\\r\\n- **另外三個 NOT_RECOMMENDED 的具體阻擋。** `chore/s4post-verification-harness` 與 `feat/s4post-triple-upgrade-20261012-r2` 分別已有 17/18、16/17 個非 merge patch 在 main 等價出現，但各剩一個非等價 CI patch，整枝仍造成 README 及七個治理檔衝突；應只評估剩餘 CI 差異。`fix/s4post-far-adjudication-20261012` 雖 merge-tree exit 0，但 head `offline-gate=failure`，同時變更 `tools/verify_evidence_digests.py`、新增 eligibility probe 與測試，且夾帶 26,154、1,569、18,160 行的三份 writer logs，不能直接放行。\\r\\n- **SUPERSEDED 也不表示可以放心重併。** `feat/s4post-triple-upgrade-20261012` 的唯一 patch 在 main 等價存在（git cherry 顯示 `- 38f9067...`），但仍對 plugin/derivations 與兩個 builder 產生 add/add 衝突。正確動作是不併，不是「修完衝突再重併」。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 建議執行順序\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **重新凍結 SHA 與 owner admission。** 真正執行前重讀兩 repo 的 `ls-remote --heads`、PR#2 head/base；若不再是 SBL main=`bedc11957d4fc2474b249b69d200829272c118ef`、head=`a6bcdd0f8f0d12ebb413679ce5c43edd763fd3a2`，本裁決須重測。讀 branch protection／required checks；本次 protection API 為 403，不能推定未受保護。\\r\\n2. **唯一整枝推薦：SBL PR#2。** Owner 以受保護 PR 流程併入 `candidate/c3-20261021-v2`；不先併 v1、不併 evidence 支線、不 force push。Gate：merge-base 與 main 相同、ahead/behind 6/0、27 檔 +2,576/−63、merge-tree exit 0、Ubuntu/Windows Python 3.11 head checks 均 completed/success，並確認 PR 核准與 required checks 無缺漏。\\r\\n3. **PR#2 本輪可重現 gate。** 在 scratch detached HEAD 重跑：`python -m pytest -p no:cacheprovider -rs` → exit 0、191 passed/3 skipped；`python tools/check_wheel_contents.py` → exit 0、wheel 39 members、八個 required runtime data 路徑全部 OK；`python tools/verify_source.py --self-check` → exit 0、14-doc manifest 自洽，但沒有 corpus byte verification。若 owner 要求所有測試不得 skip，需在可建 symlink 且有 jsonschema 的環境補跑；禁止把 skip 算 PASS。\\r\\n4. **併入後回讀，而非只相信按鈕。** 檢查 PR merged 狀態、main 新 SHA 及候選 SHA ancestry（採 merge commit 時）；若採 squash，則比較預期合併 tree 與實際 tree，不能用 ancestry 假裝能驗證 squash。重跑 main CI、wheel runtime data 與 source manifest gate；不因此把 `PACKAGED_HOST_PENDING`、`PARTIAL_CHALLENGE` 或 `FAIL_FULL_CONTRACT` 自動升級。\\r\\n5. **PIPD 現階段不推薦任何整枝 merge。** 對已併 ancestry 分支或全部 patch-equivalent 分支不做重複併入；保留 evidence SHA。若要救回 NOT_RECOMMENDED 的一個 CI patch或 FAR validator 修補，從當時最新 main 建立新的最小 scoped PR，而非重用舊分支；先檢視 main 已有相同行為與 mutation fixtures，再補 head CI 與 integration merge-tree；required gates 未全過不得合併。\\r\\n6. **release 與治理資料另案。** 對 preview.3/preview.4 的 tag、asset name、digest、tuple 變更需獨立 Release Owner admission 與下載 bytes 重算；不得移動既有 tag、覆蓋 release asset 或把歷史 tuple 重寫成目前 main 的身分。純證據只用固定 SHA 引用；確有主線文件需求時另做窄版索引 PR。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 限制\\r\\n\\r\\n- 本輪完成 SBL 12、PIPD-LS-SP 37 個非 main 遠端分支，共 49 個；遠端含 main 分別為 13、38 個。原先「約 37 分支」不是 37 個含 main 的上限。本輪覆核 `ls-remote` 與開始快照相同。\\r\\n- 使用隔離 bare clones、scratch detached checkout 與 GitHub REST GET；沒有 push、merge、tag、release，也未變更原 SBL 工作樹或原有分支。初讀原工作樹已有 `?? _governance/round-20261021-r7/`；末讀另見 `?? openspec/changes/sbl-v01-candidate-build/R7_DELTA.md`，顯示有本子任務以外的並行寫入，不能宣稱整個工作樹狀態完全不變。本子任務只寫 scratch，未碰觸這些檔案。\\r\\n- 統計定義是 `git diff --stat main...head` 與 `--numstat`（merge-base→候選的三點差異）；`ahead/behind` 是候選/main 的獨有提交數。檔案數包含 binary，但 +/− 只計文字行，binary 的 `-` 不當作零內容。已併祖先顯示 0 檔，是無候選增量，不是與 main tip bytes 完全相同；另存 `main_tip_stat`。\\r\\n- `git merge-tree --write-tree main head` 僅寫 scratch object database、不建 merge commit、不改 refs/index/工作樹；exit 0 為無衝突、1 為有衝突，不是功能驗收通過。方法依 Git 官方文件；patch 等價依 `git cherry`，不是僅憑分支名稱判斷。\\r\\n- 無 gh CLI；以 token-authenticated urllib 讀取各 head SHA 的 statuses、check-runs、Actions runs，無 API 錯誤或達到 100 筆分頁上限。表內 CI 保留可見 check 名稱及 conclusion；`NO_RUNS` 不是 PASS。legacy statuses 全為 pending/0，不把它誤當有工作執行中；以實際 check-runs 與 Actions conclusions 判讀。未下載完整 CI job logs，沒有假稱重跑所有 PIPD 測試。\\r\\n- `/rules/branches/main` 對兩 repo 回傳空陣列，但 `/branches/main/protection` 皆 HTTP 403；required checks／review protection 完整狀態為 NOT_VERIFIED，而非「沒有規則」。\\r\\n- SBL 三個 skip：兩個需要 Windows symlink 權限，一個缺 jsonschema。初次 archive-only 測試因缺 Git metadata 失敗，加入 scratch detached Git metadata 後通過；wheel 初次遇 Windows 長路徑，再跑遇殘留 build directory，改用全新 scratch source 與 extended-length TEMP 後通過。初次失敗及成功重跑均保留，未以編造結果取代。\\r\\n- 八個 PIPD skills 的存在及文本已逐一讀取；未宣稱八個技能在本主機已實際載入／獨立驗收／端到端執行成功。未把 2026-10-16 舊 FAR 裁決當成本輪 Git 證據。\\r\\n- 原始證據、精確 SHA、每分支 merge-base、top-level 分布及 conflict
